   \"max_entropy_bits\": max_entropy,\n        \"n_layers\": n_layers,\n        \"n_heads\": n_heads,\n        \"per_layer_entropy_ratio\": {str(i): round(float(per_layer_ratio[i]), 4) for i in range(len(per_layer_ratio))},\n        \"per_layer_entropy_std\": {str(i): round(float(per_layer_ratio_std[i]), 4) for i in range(len(per_layer_ratio_std))},\n        \"per_layer_vision_attention_fraction\": {str(i): round(float(per_layer_vis_frac[i]), 4) for i in range(len(per_layer_vis_frac))},\n        \"overall_entropy_ratio\": round(overall_ratio, 4),\n        \"overall_entropy_std\": round(overall_ratio_std, 4),\n        \"overall_vision_attention_fraction\": round(float(all_vision_attn_frac.mean()), 4),\n        \"min_entropy_layer\": {\"layer\": min_layer, \"ratio\": round(float(per_layer_ratio[min_layer]), 4)},\n        \"max_entropy_layer\": {\"layer\": max_layer, \"ratio\": round(float(per_layer_ratio[max_layer]), 4)},\n    }\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Part B: Pruning Curve (L2-norm, hidden state degradation)\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\", flush=True)\n\n    pruning_losses = {k: [] for k in KEEP_RATIOS}\n\n    for idx in range(n_images):\n        img = images[idx]\n        task = task_names[idx]\n        prompt = get_prompt_for_task(task)\n        inputs = processor(prompt, img).to(DEVICE, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n        hook.keep_ratio = 1.0\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            out_full = model(\n                **inputs,\n                output_hidden_states=True,\n                return_dict=True,\n            )\n        h_full = out_full.hidden_states[-1][:, 1+N_VISION_TOKENS:, :].float()\n        h_full_norm_sq = (h_full ** 2).sum().item()\n\n        pruning_losses[1.0].append(0.0)\n\n        for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n            if k >= 1.0:\n                continue\n            hook.keep_ratio = k\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                out_pruned = model(\n                    **inputs,\n                    output_hidden_states=True,\n                    return_dict=True,\n                )\n            h_pruned = out_pruned.hidden_states[-1][:, 1+N_VISION_TOKENS:, :].float()\n            degradation = ((h_pruned - h_full) ** 2).sum().item()\n            normalized = degradation / max(h_full_norm_sq, 1e-10)\n            pruning_losses[k].append(normalized)\n\n            del out_pruned\n            torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n        del out_full\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\n        if (idx + 1) % 10 == 0 or idx == 0:\n            summary = {k: f\"{np.mean(v):.6f}\" for k, v in pruning_losses.items() if v}\n            print(f\"  [{idx+1}/{n_images}] losses={summary}\", flush=True)\n\n    pruning_results = {}\n    for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n        vals = pruning_losses[k]\n        pruning_results[str(k)] = {\n            \"normalized_loss\": round(float(np.mean(vals)), 6),\n            \"std\": round(float(np.std(vals)), 6),\n            \"n_tokens_kept\": max(1, int(N_VISION_TOKENS * k)),\n        }\n\n    print(f\"\\n[PRUNING RESULTS]\")\n    print(f\"{'k':>6} {'tokens':>6} {'norm_loss':>12} {'std':>10}\")\n    for k in KEEP_RATIOS:\n        r = pruning_results[str(k)]\n        print(f\"  {k:.2f}   {r['n_tokens_kept']:>4}   {r['normalized_loss']:>10.6f}   {r['std']:>8.6f}\")\n\n    handle.remove()\n\n    return entropy_results, pruning_results\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"OpenVLA-OFT Token Importance Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\" \"hidden_state_degradation_normalized\",\n        \"description\": \"MSE(H_pruned - H_full) / ||H_full||^2 at text token positions\",\n        \"keep_ratios\": {str(k): pruning_results[str(k)] for k Only model-00004 is properly downloaded (no .aria2 file).\n\nThis also explains my earlier \"weights differ\" test — I was comparing a weight that happened to be in a partially-downloaded region of model-00002. \n\nThe base OpenVLA has non-zero values there, while the OFT file has zeros in those undownloaded sections. Looking at the download sequence, the smaller files like proprio_projector and lora_adapter are being prioritized, but the large model shards (1-3) that are actually incomplete should have been downloaded first.\n\nThe script's completion check is looking for three conditions: the file exists, there's no .aria2 file present, and the file size meets expectations. Since those .aria2 files are still there from the previous download attempt, the check fails and should trigger a re-download, but the script hasn't reached those large shards yet in the queue.\n\nI'm calculating the remaining download time: about 14.7GB at 80KB/s would take roughly 52 hours, which is impractical. But I'm second-guessing whether the weights are actually merged—the earlier test showing diff=0.486 might have been a false positive since it was comparing a partially downloaded region against one that hadn't started yet.\n\nThe real question is whether these OFT safetensors contain just the base model weights or if they're already merged with the LoRA deltas. Rather than waiting for the full download, I could try applying the LoRA adapter manually using PEFT, or look for faster mirrors like ModelScope.\n\nSince the base OpenVLA model is already correctly loaded at `/root/autodl-tmp/openvla-libero-object/`, I could skip the massive download and instead load that base model, then apply the 462MB LoRA adapter file that's queued next.
